1b1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya ku shal mU la ba ri d+ha ra nA ma ma hA yA na su tra/bod skad du/
1b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'phags pa dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su 'dzin pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//bam po dang po//sangs rgyas dang 
1b3(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 
1b4(mdo sna tshogs): 'das rgyal po'i khab na 'od ma'i tshal byag lan dkag nas phan 'di lta ste/bsam gtan pa rnams kyi gnas/
1b5(mdo sna tshogs): gnas med pa rnams kyi gnas/stong pa nyid la gnas pa rnams kyi gnas/mtshan ma med pa la gnas 
1b6(mdo sna tshogs): pa dang /smon pa med pa la gnas pa rnams kyi gan san 'di lta ste/dge slong 'bum gyi dge slong gi dge 
1b7(mdo sna tshogs): 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs ste/thams cad kyang sgra bskyungs pa/dben pa la gnas 
1b8(mdo sna tshogs): pa/nad du yang dag 'jog la mngon par brtson pa sha stag go/de'i tshe tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa 
2a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
2b1(mdo sna tshogs): pa de dang 'dra ba'i byang chub sems dpa' stong dang lhan cig tu yul tsam par dbyar gnas nas lam du zhugs te/rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa ga la ba dang /bcom ldan 'das ga la 
2b2(mdo sna tshogs): ba der dong ste lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//yang de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bzang skyong dang /dkon mchog 'byung 
2b3(mdo sna tshogs): gnas dang /ded dpon bzang po dang /phug spas dang /mis byin dang /dbang pos byin dang /chu lha dang /tshe ngas lha dang /stobs bzangs dang /bla ma'i blo gros dang /khyad par blo gros dang /'phel ba'i blo gros 
2b4(mdo sna tshogs): dang /mthong ba don yod dang /legs par yang dag zhugs dang /rab kyi rtsal gyis rnam par gnon dang /rtag tu brtson dang /brtson pa mi gtong dang /nyi ma'i snying po dang /'gro ba 'dzin dang /sa 'dzin dang /
2b5(mdo sna tshogs): bdud rtsi 'dzin dang /blo gros rab gnas dang /blo gros mtha' yas dang /brtan pa'i blo gros dang /'jig rten gsum rnam par gnon dang /mtha' yas rnam par gnon dang /dpag med rnam par gnon dang /
2b6(mdo sna tshogs): rdo rje'i gom pas rnam par gnon dang /don yod pa'i gom pas rnam par gnod dang /mi g.yo ba'i gom pas rnam par gnonadang /spobs pa chen po dang /spobs pa rnon po dang /spobs pa zab pa dang /spobs pa 
2b7(mdo sna tshogs): mtha' yas dang /spobs pa dpag med dang /'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang /pad ma dpal gyi snying po dang /chos 'phags dang /lag na rin chen dang /rin chen 'dzin dang /bskal pa 'gyur ba mtha' yas 
2b8(mdo sna tshogs): par go cha bgos pa dang /bud med 'gyur ba'i go cha bgos pa dang /skyes pa 'gyur ba'i go cha bgos pas dang /sems can 'gyur ba'i go cha bgos dang /mtha' yas khyu mchog dang /dpag med khyu mchog dang /
3a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//dmigs pa thams cad rnam par 'jig pa'i khyu mchog dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag thams cad kyang tha mi dad par spyod pa'i go cha bgos pa sha stag ste/yul tha dad pa rnams 
3a2(mdo sna tshogs): su dbyar gnas par gnas nas yul rgyu zhing gcig tu 'dus te/rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa ga la ba dang /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das 
3a3(mdo sna tshogs): kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po de dag 'dus par thugs su chud nas rdzu 'phrul ji lta bu'i 
3a4(mdo sna tshogs): mngon par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad pas rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan ka gnas pa na/dge slong dang /dge slong ma dang /dge bsnyen dang /dge bsnyen ma ji snyed gnas pa de dag 
3a5(mdo sna tshogs): thams cad bcom ldan 'das la blta ba dang phyag 'tshal ba dang /bsnyen bskur bya ba'i phyir 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan ga gnas pa ga la ba dang bcom ldan 'das ga la bder dong ste lhags nas//bcom ldan 'das 
3a6(mdo sna tshogs): kyi zhabasal mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/phyogs gcig tu 'khod par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba mngonapar 'du mdzad do//de'i tshe tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po lus 'phags ri'i dbang po'i brag 
3a7(mdo sna tshogs): phug na dge slong lnga brgya tham pa thams cad kyang dgon pa ba/bsod snyoms pa/phyag dar khrod pa/chos gos gsum pa/cog bu pa/rtswa'i gding pa ba/shing drung pa/'dod pa chung ba/chog shes pa/rab tu dben 
3a8(mdo sna tshogs): pa ba/chos gos ngan pa 'chang bsha stag dang lhan cig tu 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rdzu 'phrul ji lta bu mngon par 'du bya ba mngo par 'du mdzad pas tshe dang ldan pa 'od srungs chen po dge slong lnga 
3b1(mdo sna tshogs): brgya tham pa de dag dang lhan cig tu lus 'phags ri'i dbang po'i brag phug nas mi snang bar gyur te/rgyal po'i khab na 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na/'dug pa de lta bu'i rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba 
3b2(mdo sna tshogs): mngon par 'du mdzad de/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po rgyang ring po nyid nas 'ong pa gzigs te/gzigs nas kyang bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag 
3b3(mdo sna tshogs): gnas brtan 'od srung chen po 'od pa 'di ni dgon pa pa/bsod snyoms pa/phyag dar khrod pa/chos gos gsum pa/chos gos ngan pa 'chad pa/bas mtha'i gnas mal la gnas pa/'dod pa chung ba/chog shes 
3b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa/rab tu dben par gnas pa/ma 'dres pa/chos thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa ste/dge slong dag nga'i nyan thos ji snyed pa ni dge slong dag da ltar dge slong 'od srung chen po'i sbyangs pa'i yon tan 
3b5(mdo sna tshogs): dag dang mtshungs pa 'am 'dra ba ni ngas yang dag par rjes su ma mthong ngo //dge slong dag dge slong 'od srung chos gos gsum pa ni lha rnams dang yang smra ba dang kun tu smra bar mi 'dod na mi rnams dang lta smos kyang 
3b6(mdo sna tshogs): ci dgos/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po rgyang ring po nas 'ong ba gzigs nas bka' stsal pa/'od srung tshur shog 'od srung khyod legs par 'ongs so//kye ma gnas brtan 'od 
3b7(mdo sna tshogs): srung gis ni 'di lta ste/'dir 'ong bar bya ba'i phyir yun ring po nas rnam grangs byas so//'od srung khyod gang na 'di'i phyed la 'dug shig cas bcom ldan 'das kyis gnas brtan 'od srung la gdan gyi phyed gnad 
3b8(mdo sna tshogs): ma thag tu de nas bcom ldan 'das gdan de las bskyod pa dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di 'gul/rab tu 'gul/kun tu rab tu 'gul/g.yos rab tu g.yos/kun tu rab tu 
4a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
4b1(mdo sna tshogs): rtog pa'i rnam par brtags pa'am/'dod chags kyi yongs su gdung bas gdungs par gyur pa'am/zhe sdang gi yongs su gdung ba dang /gti mug gi yongs su gdung bas yongs su gdungs par gyur par ma dran 
4b2(mdo sna tshogs): lags so//bcom ldan 'das mdor na slob par gyur pa bdag gis bcom ldan 'das las sha na'i ras phyag dar khrod mnos pa rnams mi slob pa'i sa la thug gi bar du spyi bos mnos lags so//bcom ldan 
4b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'das bdag gis longs spyod pa'i slad du blangs pa ni ma lags kyi gdams pa dang /rjes su bstan pa las brtsams nas blangs lags so//bcom ldan 'da'as de dag blangs nas sems mtho ba'am dma' 
4b4(mdo sna tshogs): bar gyur par mi dran lags so//bcom ldan 'dis sha na'i ras phyag dar khrid de dag chos gos ma mchis pa'i lus la sbyar bar mi dran lags so//bco ma ldan 'das sha na'i ras phyag dar khro de dag sug pa ma red pas 
4b5(mdo sna tshogs): reg par mi dran lags so/bcom ldan 'das gang dang gang du mchi ba de dang der sha na'i ras phyag dar khrod/de dag mgos 'tshal cing mchis lags so//bcom ldana'adas sha na'i ras phyag dang khrod de dag yan 
4b6(mdo sna tshogs): lags mi gtsang ba gang gis kyang reg par mi dran lags so////bcom ldan 'das sha na'i ras pa phyag dar khrod mchod rten du gyur pa de dag bcangs lags so//bcom ldan
5a1(mdo sna tshogs): rten pha rol gyi 'du shes su ma gyur pa dang /bcom ldan 'das gang mthong ba dang /thos pa dang /bye brag phyed pa dang /rnam par shes pa dang /yid kyis rjes su brtags pa dang /rjes su bsams pa de la 
5a2(mdo sna tshogs): yang bdag 'du shes su ma gyur pa dag bcom ldan 'das gang 'du shes med pa'i gnas pa dang /'du shes med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa'i gnas pa dang /gang 'du shes las yang dag par 'da' ba'i gnas pa dang /
5a3(mdo sna tshogs): gang 'du shes med pa las yang dag par 'da' pa'i gnas pa dang /gangs 'du shes kyi gnas pa dang /gang 'ang du shes med pa'i gnas pa/gang 'du shes thams cad las yang dag par 'da' ma'i gnas pa de yang slob 
5a4(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i gnas pa dang /mi slob pa'i gnas par bdag gis yang dag par rjes su ma mthong lags so//bcom ldan 'das gnas pa de la de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag par rjes su mthong /de bzhin gshegs 
5a5(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i chos rnams dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas pa yang dag par rjes mthong lags te/bdag ni de ltar gnas lags so//bcom ldan dang 'das dper bgyi na nam mkha' bla gab ma mchis pa la ming 
5a6(mdo sna tshogs): dang brda' dang /gdags pa rnam pa sna tshogs bgyis pa dag bcom ldan 'das ming dang brda' dang gdags pa gang dag gis nam mkha' zhes tha snyad du btags pa dang /nam mkha' zhes brdar brtags pa dang 
5a7(mdo sna tshogs): nam mkha' zhes rten du bgyis pa dang /bcom ldan 'das nam mkha' zhe'am/bla gab ma mchis pa zhe'am/stong pa zhe'am/gsog ce'am/gsob ce'am/ya ma rla zhe'am/gzung ba ma mchis pa zhe'am/tshad 
5a8(mdo sna tshogs): ma mchis pa zhe'am/rten pa ma mchis pa zhe'am/blang ba ma mchis pa zhe'am/dor ba ma mchis pa zhe'am/lus ma mchis pa zhe'am/ji lta ba bzhin zhe'am/yongs su dag pa zhe'am/bar snang zhe'am/thogs 
5b1(mdo sna tshogs): pa ma mchis pa zhe 'am/dngos po ma mchis pa zhe 'am/bstan du ma mchis pa zhes de dag dang /gzhan yang tha snyid kyi brda' tha dad pa rnam pa sna tshogs kyis brjod pa ni bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste/tshad 
5b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'di tsam 'am/yongs su bcad pa 'di tsam 'am/mnyam pa 'am/mi mnyam pa zhes ming 'am/brda' 'am/rten rnams kyis 'tshal bar mi nus lags so//bcom ldan 'das gang dag de'i phyogs tsam zhig 
5b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'tshal ba de dag thams cad kyis kyang kha dog gam//dbyibs sam dmigs pas nam mkha' de chad 'di tsam dang /mchog 'di tsam zhes bgyi ba ma 'tshal lags so//bcom ldan 'angs de bzhin du gang de bzhin 
5b4(mdo sna tshogs): gshegs pa zhes bgyi ba 'am/sangs rgyas zhes bgyi ba 'am/ston pa zhe 'am/skyabs zhe lam/dpung gnyen zhe 'am/gnas zhe 'am/'dren pa zhe 'am/rnam par 'dren pa zhe 'am/yongs su 'dren pa zhe 'am/sman 
5b5(mdo sna tshogs): pa zhe 'am/gso ba zhe 'am/lam ston pa zhe 'am/lam brjod pa zhe 'am/gzhan yang tha snyad tha dad pa'i tshig rnam pa sna tshogs brjod pa'i tha snyad kyi tshig gang dag gis mkhas pa rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das bstod pa 
5b6(mdo sna tshogs): dang /bkur ba dang /bsngags pa dang /rab tu bstod pa dang /rab tu bsngags pa dang /bcom ldan 'das tha snyad kyi tshig gang dag gas 'jig rten mngon par brjod pa'i chos de dag ni bcom ldan 'das bdag ma 'tshal 
5b7(mdo sna tshogs): mi rten ma mthong lags so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad ni ngo bo nyid kyis ya ma rla dang /ngo bo nyid kyis gso ba lags pa'i slad du'o//bcom ldan 'das dper bgyi na sgyu ma mkhan gyis 
5b8(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba/dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa dang ldan pa/rin po che sna bdun dang ldan pa'i 'khor los sgyur ba zhig sprul par gyur la/bcom ldan 'das de'i thad du gling bzhi 
6a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can rnam par rgyal bar bgyi ba la gnas pa/mtha' yas pa de thams cad lhags shing bcom ldan 'das de la lus tha dad par yang gda' la/
6a2(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das de la sems can lus tha dad par yang mthong lags mod kyi/bcom ldan 'das sgyu ma mkhan gyis sprul pa'i 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po de 'di snyam du bdag gis ni dpung gi tshogs 'di'i bdag 
6a3(mdo sna tshogs): po bya zhing /gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rjes su 'jug par bya'o snyam du mi sems lags la/dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi po de yang 'di snyam du 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal bo 'di ni bdag cag gi gtso 
6a4(mdo sna tshogs): bo yin pas bdag cag ni 'di'i rjes su 'jug par bya'o snya du mi sems lags mod kyi/de dag nga'i rjes su 'jug par bgyid par yang gda' lags so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du chos de dag gi chos nyid 
6a5(mdo sna tshogs): gang lags pa de la/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'am/nyan thos sam/slob pa 'am/mi slob pa'am/rang sangs rgyas sam/so so'i skye bo ma mchis lags so//bcom ldan 
6a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'das chos nyid de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyid dmigs pa'am/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos ny-id mngon pa'am/de bzhin gshegs ba'i chos nyid rten pa'am/rang sangs rgyas kyi chos nyid dmigs pa 'am/rang sangs 
6a7(mdo sna tshogs): rgyas kyi chos nyid mngon pa'am/rang sangs rgyasakyi chos nyid rten pa'am/nyan thos kyi chos nyid dmigs ba'am/nyan thos kyi chos nying mngon pa'am/nyan thos kyi chosanyid brten pa'am/so so'i skye bo'i chos 
6a8(mdo sna tshogs): nyid dmigs pa'am/so so'i skye bo'i cho sa nyid mngon pa'am/so so'i skye bo'ichos nyid brten pa'am/gzugs kyi chos nyid dmigs pa'am/gzugs kyi chos nyid mngon pa 'am/gzugs kyi chos nyid
6b1(mdo sna tshogs): brten pa 'am/de bzhin du tshor ba dang /'du shes dang /'du byed dang /rnam par shes pa'i chos nyid dmigs pa 'am/rnam par shes pa'i chos nyer mngon pa 'am/rnam par shes ba'i chos nyid brtan par mi 'gyur 
6b2(mdo sna tshogs): lags so//bcom ldan 'das chos nyid de la gzugs stong pa dang /'dis gzugs stong pa dang /'di la gzugs stong pa dmigs pa dang /de bzhin du tshor ba dang/'du shes dang /'du byad dang /rnam par shes
6b3(mdo sna tshogs): pa stong pa dang /'dis rnam par shes pa stong pa dang /'di la rnam par shes pa stong ba dmigs pa dang /de bzhan du de bzhin gshegs pa stong pa dang /'dis de bzhin gshegs pa stod pa dang /'di la de bzhin gshegs
6b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa stong pa dmigs pa dang/de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyid stong pa dmigs pa dang 'dis de bzhin gshegs pa'i chosanyid stong pa dang /'di la de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos
6b5(mdo sna tshogs): nyid stong pa dmigs par mi 'gyur lags so//bcom ldan 'angs de bzhin du de la nyan thos ma mchis/nyan thos kyi chos nyid ma mchis/so so'i skye bo ma mchis/so so'i skye bo'i chos nyid kyi bar du ma mchis lags so//bcom ldan
6b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'das dper bgyi na/sgyu ma mkhan gyis 'khor los sgyur ba 'am/dpung gi tshogs sprul pa de la 'khor los sgyur ba ma mchis/dpung gi tshogs ma mchis lags so//sgyu ma la ma mchis/sgyu ma'i chos nyid la
6b7(mdo sna tshogs): ma mchis/sgyu ma mkhan rnams la ma mchis lags so//sa la ma mchis/chu la ma mchis/me la ma mchis/rlung la ma mchis/nam mkha' la ma mchis/rnam par shes pa la ma mchis lags so/sa
6b8(mdo sna tshogs): dang /chu dang /me dang /rlung dang/nam mkha'i khams rnams la ma mchis//rnam par shes pa'i khams la ma mchis lags so //bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad ni de dang 'dra lags te/bcom ldan 'das de
7a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//dag la bdag ni rlom sems su mi bgyid/de dag la bdag ni byung bar ma gyur/de dag bdag gis ni ma smras lags so//bcom ldan 'das de ltar bdag ni yon tan gyi chos rnams nyid las dben 
7a2(mdo sna tshogs): pa lags pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rnams rjes su dran par bgyid lags so//bcom ldan 'dis de ni lam lags/de ni tshul lags te/lam de la gnas pa'i rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo lam de 
7a3(mdo sna tshogs): rjes su thob pa rnams ni 'di snyam du gzhan yang dag par blangs la 'jug par bya zhing /ston pa yang gzhan btsal bar bya la/dge sbyong ngam/bram ze gzhan smra ba'i gdong du gyen du blta bar bya zhing /tshe dang ldan pa 'di ni 
7a4(mdo sna tshogs): shes bzhin pas shes/mthong bzhin pas mthong /yang dag pa'i lta ba yang 'dogs bzhin pas 'dogs par byed do snyam du mi sems lags te/de ni gnas ma mchis lags so//bcom ldan 
7a5(mdo sna tshogs): 'das gnas pa 'di la ni bdag som nyi ma mchis pa thob pas gnas lags so//chos kyi sgo de yang rjes su thob pas gnas lags so//chos thams cad ni 'di lta ste dben pa'i tshul dang /skye ba ma mchis pa'i tshul dag gis tshul gcig pa 
7a6(mdo sna tshogs): lags so//bcom ldan 'das bdag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la chos kyi the tshom zhu bar 'tshal ba lags so//de bzhin gshegs pas kyang bdag la skabs phye ba 
7a7(mdo sna tshogs): lags te/dbang po'i brag phug nas 'od ma'i tshal 'dir phyin pa lags so//bcom ldan 'da'as bdag 'dir mchis pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis gdan gyi phyed gnad ste/bdag la gnang ba na stong gsum gyi stong 
7a8(mdo sna tshogs): chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di rab tu 'ur 'ur zhing rnam pa drug tu yang g.yos rab tu g.yos lags so//bcom ldan 
7b1(mdo sna tshogs): rgya chen po'i chos dang ldan pa de lta bu dang /slob dpon ma mchis shing rang byung ba lags kyang thugs rje chen po'i tshul la bzhugs shing /lhag pa'i nga rgyal ma mchis pa dang /nga rgyal ma mchis pa la bzhugs nas 
7b2(mdo sna tshogs): bdag la gdan gyi phyed gnang ba ngo mtshar lags so snyam bgyid de/bcom ldan 'das dper bgyi na mi dbul po 'tsho ba ngan pa tshogs kyis 'tsho ba zhig tshogs kyis khyim gyi phun sum tshogs pa bsgrubs nas/
7b3(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bo nas dbang bskur ba dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang ldan pa'i gan du blta ba dang phyag bgyi ba dang /bsnyen bkur bgyi ba dang /dri bar bgyi ba'i slad du mchis par gyur la/mchis pa de la 
7b4(mdo sna tshogs): des stan gyi phyed stsal na/de nas de 'di snyam du sems lags te/rgyal po la blta ba dang /phyag bya ba'i phyir bdag song du zin kyang bdag gis blta ba dang /phyag bya ba yang dka' bar 'gyur na gsol ba lta ci smos/
7b5(mdo sna tshogs): rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi po nas dbang bskur bas bdag phyin ma thag pa la stan gyi phyed byin du zin kyang 'di na ngo mtshar ro snyam bgyid lags so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du chos kyi rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa 
7b6(mdo sna tshogs): dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ni rang byung ba/slob dpon ma mchis pa/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas rnams kyis kyang bsnyen par dka' zhing rtogs par dka' ba lags na/lha dang mi dang /lha 
7b7(mdo sna tshogs): ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyis lta smos kyang ci 'tshal/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la chos kyi the tshom zhu ba dang /blta ba dang /phyag bgyi ba 
7b8(mdo sna tshogs): dang /bsnyen bkur bgyi ba'i slad du bdag ji lta ji ltar mchis pa na/bcom ldan 'das bdag 'di snyam du sems lags te/bdag gis ni de bzhin gshegs pa lta ba rnyed cing phyag bgyi ba rnyed la bsnyen bkur bgyi 
8a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
8b1(mdo sna tshogs): tshad de lta bu dang /rnam par grol ba'i tshad de lta bu dang /rnam bar grol ba'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshad de lta bu dang /rna bar grol ba'i ye shes mthong ba'i tshad de lta bu dang rnam par grol ba'i ye shes mthong pa'i pha 
8b2(mdo sna tshogs): rol tu phyin pa'i tshad de blta bus dpag par mi nus so//'od srung bzhi po 'di dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mi mnyam pa'i ye shes rnams yin te/de dag dang ldan na de bzhin gshegs pa 'khor gyi nang du seng 
8b3(mdo sna tshogs): ge'i sgra sgrogs par byed pa'o//bzhi gang zhe na/tshul khrims mnyam pa nyid dang /ting nge 'dzin mnyam pa nyid dang /shes rab mnyam pa nyid dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos mnyam pa nyid do//'od srung mi mnyam pa'i 
8b4(mdo sna tshogs): ye shes bzhi po de dag ni gang dag dang ldan na de bzhin/gshegs pa 'khor gyi nang na bzhugs shing seng ge'i sgra yang dag par sgrogs par byed pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di 
8b5(mdo sna tshogs): dag bka' stsal to//sangs rgyas 'jig rten bsam yas na//sangs rgyas ye shes mtha' yas te//sems kyi las ni sbyangs pas na//de dag seng ge sgra sgrogs byed//seng ge'i sgra sgrogs byed pa ni//mu stegs 
8b6(mdo sna tshogs): gzhan rnams skrag par 'gyur//chos kyi chos nyid thos nas su//g.yang sar rab tu ltung bar 'gyur//bdag tu 'du shes gang gnas dang //sems can 'du shes gang gnas pa//'od srung de dag bstan 'di la//
8b7(mdo sna tshogs): mu stegs can zhes ngas bshad do//dngos po'i 'du shes gang gnas dang gang yin zhes brten pa rnams//'od srung de dag bsta na 'di la//mu stegs can zhes ngas bshang ngo //gang dag tshul khrims phung 
8b8(mdo sna tshogs): po dang //bsam gtan rnams dang thos mang gis//rlom sems rnams rmongs de dag ni//'od srung mu stegs can rnams so//bdag ni chog shes 'dod chung dang //rab tu dben la gnas pa dang //chos 
9a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//gos ngan pa 'chang snyam pa//'od srung de ni mu stegs so//dper na nam mkha' 'di la ni//lag pa bskyod kyang thogs med cing //dper na mkha' la rdul gyis kyang //nam yang gos par mi 'gyur 
9a2(mdo sna tshogs): ba//de bzhin 'od srung ngas bstan pa'i//chos 'dir gang dag gnas de dag/nyon mongs rnams kyis mi gos te//mkha' las rdul dang sprin bzhin no//ji ltar mkha' la mi yis me tog spos rnams dang //
9a3(mdo sna tshogs): de bzhin phreng ba byug pa rnams kyis 'dir mchod kyang /bdag mchod la la skye 'gyur 'jig par 'gyur ro zhes//de la blo gros kyang ni 'byung bar mi 'gyur dang //ji ltar nam mkha' de la gos par mi 'gyur dang //
9a4(mdo sna tshogs): ji ltar de yi rang bzhin gos par mi 'gyur dang //ji ltar de yi rang bzhin ngo bo nyid kyis gsob/de bzhin dge sbyong dag la stong pa'i chos rnams so//ji ltar gang gis nam mkha' bsdigs pa dang //ji ltar nam du 
9a5(mdo sna tshogs): yang ni gshe bar mi 'gyur ba//de bzhin dge sbyong dag la chos rnams gang dag yin//de dag nam mkha' bzhin du gos pa med chos rnams//chos 'dir dge sbyong gang dag legs par bslabs rnams dang //gzhan 
9a6(mdo sna tshogs): dag slob pa rnams dang slob par 'gyur ba rnams//'di la nam du'ang gang dag chags par mi 'gyur ba//nga yis de dag dge sbyong legs bslabs rnams su bstan//ji ltar mkha' la nam yang lag pa mi thogs dang //
9a7(mdo sna tshogs): rdul dang du ba rnams kyis gos par mi 'gyur dang / /ji ltar lam ni nam yang byed par mi 'gyur ba//chos la de bzhin mos pa rnams ni dge sbyong yin//ji ltar sprin dang bral bar gyur pa'i nam mkha' la//zla ba thogs 
9a8(mdo sna tshogs): pa med par mkha' la 'gro byed kyang //bdag gis bar snang la ni snang bar bya snyam du//de la rnam rtog de ni 'byung bar mi 'gyur bzhin//rgod med bdag nyid dge slong zla ba lta bu yis//sbyin pa'i bdag po'i khyim rnams 
9b1(mdo sna tshogs): su ni 'gro bar bya//rgod med nga rgyal rnam grol sems dang ldan pa dang //'jig rten chos rnams kyis ni ma gos sems ldan bya//khyim rnams la ni nga rgyal spangs nas bsten par bya//der phyin nas kyang chos nyid rab tu 
9b2(mdo sna tshogs): brjod par bya//bde dang rnyed pa'i phyir ni 'dod pa'ang spangs nas su//'jig rten rnam dag tshul khrims can du gnas par bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po la bka' stsal pa/'od srung 
9b3(mdo sna tshogs): longs shig/longs la rang gi stan la 'dug shig/'od srung khyod la chos kyi the tshom ji snyed yod pa de dag de bzhin gshegs pa la dris shig dang /'od srung ngas dris pa lan btab pas khyod kyi sems rangs par bya'o//
9b4(mdo sna tshogs): de nas tshe dang ldan pa 'od srud chen po sa'i steng de nas langs te/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'dasakyis yang rdzu 'phrul ji lta bu mngon par 'du 
9b5(mdo sna tshogs): bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad pas 'dzam bu'i gling 'di na dge slong dang /dge slong ma dang /dge bsnyen dang /dge bsnyen ma ji snyed gnas pa de dag thams cad rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa ga la ba dang /
9b6(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis zhabs la mgo bos phyag mtshal te phyogs gcig tu 'khod nas/dge slong dang /dge slong dang /dge bsnyen dang dge bsnyen ma dang /lha dang /klu 
9b7(mdo sna tshogs): dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po gang dag yin pa de dag kyang 'od ma'i tshal bya ka land ka gnas par bzhugs te/de dag zhugs nas mtha' yas shing 
9b8(mdo sna tshogs): dpag tu med par shes par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang rdzu 'phrul ji lta bu mngon par 'du bya ba mngo par 'du mdzad pas stong gsum gyi 
10a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
10b1(mdo sna tshogs): gshegs pa de la bzhugs nas the tshom thams cad gcod pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs yang dag par ston par 'gyur/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa sgrub pa'i spyod pa yang dag par ston par gyur/
10b2(mdo sna tshogs): sems can thams cad yang dag par tshim pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs yang de dag par ston par 'gyur /sems can thams cad chos la yang dag par gzhol ba'i tshul gyi sgo rgya mtsho'i phyag rgya yang dag par ston par
10b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'gyur/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi spyod pa bsgrub pa yang dag par ston pa 'gyur/sangs rgyas kyi chos sgrub pa yang dag par ston bar 'gyur/sems can yongs su smin par bya ba
10b4(mdo sna tshogs): sgrub pa yang dag par ston 'gyur/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa sgrub pa yang dag par stong par 'gyur ba nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sgrub ba'i bar du yang dag par ston par 'byur/chos thams cad kyi tshul 
10b5(mdo sna tshogs): gyi sgo sgrub pa yang dag par stod par 'gyur/sems can 'thams cad kyi dbang po bsgrub pa yang dag par bston par 'gyur/sems can thams cad kyi gnas pa'i spyod pa sgrub pa yang dag paraston par yang 'gyur
10b6(mdo sna tshogs): 'khor bzhi yang dag par tshim par byed par 'gyur/lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po'i 'khor kyang yang dag par tshim bar byed
10b7(mdo sna tshogs): bar 'gyur/'das pa'i dus la ma chags mthogs pa'i ye shes mthong ba dang /de bzhin du da ltar dang /ma 'ongs ba'i dus la ma chags ma thogs pa'i ye shes mthong pa dang /las kyi rnam par smin pa dang /
10b8(mdo sna tshogs): spyod pa dang /smon lam rnams dang /ye shes kyi phyogs tsam zhig yang dag par ston par gyur ro//de nas tshe dang ldan pa mo'u gal gyi bu chen po stan las langs te/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos
11a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//phyag 'tshal nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi gdan 'phang du tshangs pa'i 'jig rten srid cig bshams so//steng gi bar snang la yang bcom ldan 'das kyi 'chags rin po che sna bdun las byas pa ka tsa li na de'i 
11a2(mdo sna tshogs): gos ltar reg na bde ba srid kyi tshad ni stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams dang ldan pa/rgyar ni dpag tshad bdun brgya dang ldan pa zhig byas so//'chags de la yang rtsig pa rim pa bdun dang /rta babs rim pa bdun dang /stegs 
11a3(mdo sna tshogs): bu rim pa bdun dang /dril bu g.yer ka'-i dra ba rim pa 'dun kyis kun nas bskor ba sprul to//'chags de'i g.yas g.yon su yang 'di lta ste/gser dang /dngul dang /shel dang /bai dUr+ya rin po che sna bzhi'i shing 
11a4(mdo sna tshogs): ljon pa dag mngon par sprul to//gser las byas pa'i shing ljon pa rnams la dngul gyi lo ma dag dang /shel gyi me tog dag dang /bai dUr+ya las byas pa'i 'bras bu dag dang /dngul las byas pa'i shing ljon 
11a5(mdo sna tshogs): pa rnams la gser gyi lo ma dag dang /bai dUr+yalas byas pa'i me tog dam pa shel las byas pa'i 'bras bu dang /bai dUr+ya las byed pa'i shing ljon pa rnams gser gyi lo ma dag dang/dngul gyi me tog dag dang 
11a6(mdo sna tshogs): shel gyi 'bras bu dag dang /shel las byas pa'i shing ljonapa rnams la gser gyi lo pa dag dang /dngul gyi me tog dag/bai dUr+ya las byas ba'i 'bras bu dag mngon par sprul to//shing ljon pa thams cad kyi bar dag 
11a7(mdo sna tshogs): tu yang yan lag brgyad dang ldan pa'i chus bkang ba'i rdzing them skas bzhi dang ldan pa/them skas de dag kyang rin po che sna bzhi gser dang /dngul dang /bai dUr+ya dang /shel las byas pa/gser gyi bye ma brdal ba ud 
11a8(mdo sna tshogs): pal dang /pad ma dang /ku mu te dang /pad ma dkar pos khebs pa/steng gi bar snang la yang rin po che sna bdun las byas pa'i dra ba'i bla re bres pa zhig mngon par sprul to//'chags de yang dar gyi lda ldi mang pos sbyangs 
11b1(mdo sna tshogs): pa bdug spos dri zhim pos gdugs pa/mi bdun gyi srid tsam du me tog sna tshogs gyis bcal bkram pa zhig mngon par sprul to//ud pa la dang /pad ma dang /ku mud dang /pad ma dkar po de dag la yang mau'u gal gyi bu 
11b2(mdo sna tshogs): dang 'dra ba'i dge slong dag mngon par sprul to//de nas tshe dang ldan pa mau'u gal gyi bu chen pos rdzu 'phrul gyis byas pa'i gdan de lta bu de mngon par sprul te/bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du phyin nas 'di skad ces gsol to//
11b3(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das bdag gis gdan bshams lags na/de'i dus la dbab par mkhyen par mdzad du gsol/de skad ces gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa mau'u ga gyi bu chen po la bka' 
11b4(mdo sna tshogs): stsal ba/mau'u gal gyi bu khyod kyis gdan bshams zin tam/bcom ldan 'das bdag gi bshams lags so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po yul mtha' yas 
11b5(mdo sna tshogs): pa la dmigs pa sgrub pa nges par ston pa la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i seng ge'i khri shoms shig/de bzhin gshegs pa de la bzhugs nas sems can thams cad yang 
11b6(mdo sna tshogs): dag par tshim par byed pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs yang dag par rab tu ston par 'gyur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po yul la mtha' yas pa la dmigs pa sgrub pa des par ston 
11b7(mdo sna tshogs): pas/bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar mnyan nas gdan bshams so//gdan de bsham pa na byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed 'dus pa de dag thams cad kyis seng ge'i gdan der rang rang gi bla gos dag bting ngo //de nas 
11b8(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das 'di snyam du dgongs te/bdag gis ni 'di lta ste/the tshom dang so ma nyi bsal ba'i phyir rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad pa gang gis byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi spyad pa 
12a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//bsgrub pa dang /'jug pa bsgrub pa dang /smon lam bsgrub pa dang /zhing bkod pa bsgrub pa dang /sems can yongs su smin par bya ba bsgrub pa gang dag yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' de dag 
12a2(mdo sna tshogs): byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnams su sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi bkod pa 'byung ba dang /nyan thos phun sum tshogs pa 'byung ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i phun sum tshogs 
12a3(mdo sna tshogs): pa 'byung ba dang /chos bstan pa phun sum tshogs pa 'byung pa dang /tshe phun sum tshogs pa dang /mdog phun sum tshogs pa dang gzugs phun sum tshogs pa dang mtshan phun sum tshogs pa dang dpe 
12a4(mdo sna tshogs): byad phun sum tshogs pa dang yang dag pa'i sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa dang /dam pa'i chos rab tu rnam par mi 'jig pa phun sum tshogs pa 'byug pa gang dag yin pa de dag thams cad rang rang gi chos gos 
12a5(mdo sna tshogs): dag las mthong par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba des des bar mngon par 'du bya'o snyam mo//de nas bcom ldan 'das dran pa dang mkhyen bzhin du gdan de la bzhugs te/bcom ldan 
12a6(mdo sna tshogs): 'das gdan de la bzhugs nas kyang yul mtha' yas pa la dmigs pa sgrub pa nges par ston pa zhes bya ba sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//de bzhin gshegs pa ting nge 'dzin la snyoms 
12a7(mdo sna tshogs): par zhugs nas/de nas de ma thag tu byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis rang rang gi bla gos ji snyed pa de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi gdan du gting ngo //de dag gis rang rang gi chos gos de dag la bdag nyid kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 
12a8(mdo sna tshogs): gi yon tan bkod pa phun sum tshogs pa nas/rab tu rnam par mi 'jig pa phun sum tshogs pa'i bar du yang mthong ngo //de nas byang chub sems dpa' de gang yang dag par tshim zhing yid rangs te/dga' ba dang /
12b1(mdo sna tshogs): dang pa la gnas nas/bcom ldan 'das la sgra skad 'thun par tshigs su gcad de bstod pa/cho ga spyod pa yon tan gnas gyur pa//chos rnams kun la dbang ldan bsnyengs mi mnga'//yon tan kun la gnas pas 
12b2(mdo sna tshogs): khyod mdzes te//rgyal ba dbang ldan ting 'dzin yang dag gnas//g.yo dang sgyu ni khyod la yod mi mnga'//nga rgyal rgod dang tshul 'chos mi mnga' ste//rig dang rnam grol dag gi spyod pa mkhyen//ting 'dzin de la gnas pas khyod 
12b3(mdo sna tshogs): mdzes so//dbang ldan bsnyengs bral ting 'dzin gnas nas su//rdzu 'phrul mi mtshungs bla na med pa bstan//chags med ye shes bstan phyir dbang ldan pa//ma 'ongs dus ni khyod kyis deng bstan to//mi g.yo go 'phang zhi 
12b4(mdo sna tshogs): ba thob snyam bgyid//de ring byang chub la ni mchis snyam bgyid//bdud dpung bcas pa rab tu bcom snyam bgyid//de ring rig pa rjes su thob snyam bgyid//rgyal bas gang gis 'dus byas stod gzigs pa'i//bla med mig 
12b5(mdo sna tshogs): ni thob par 'gyur snyam bgyid//chags med ye shes khyod kyi bka' drin la//mig kyang chags pa mchis pa yod ma lags//rnam par mthong yang ci yang ma mthong zhing //chos ni gang la'ang zhe 'gras ma mchis te//gang 
12b6(mdo sna tshogs): la yid ni zhe 'gras ma mchis pa//de ni sangs rgyas spyan mchog de lags so//ji ltar rgyal ba'i sangs rgyas spyan mchog gis//khams gsum shes la ma chags 'jug pa bzhin//dngos dang dngos med mnyam mtshungs 
12b7(mdo sna tshogs): yid dang ldan//yang dag brtson zhing rang gi mig la gnas//gang tshe rgyal ba ting 'dzin der gnas te//de ring de dag kun gyis thob snyam bgyid//de dag gis ni gzungs sgo de dag dang //chos la rnam par grol ba'i sgo mchog 
12b8(mdo sna tshogs): thob//sgor 'jug yongs su sbyang byas rnam dag pa//rgyal ba'i blo ni yod ye nyams mi 'gyur//sbyin pa chen po'i rnam smin de 'dra ste//khrims chen yon tan de dag de 'dra lags//shes rab chen po'i yon tan de 'dra ste//sngon chad 
13a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//
13b1(mdo sna tshogs): pa sgrub pa'i tshig rnams zhu bar 'dod pa yin/smra ba thams cad tshar gcad pa sgrub pa'i tshig rnams dang //rim par phye ba'i tshig rnams dang //chos thams cad bsdu pa'i tshig rnams zhu bar 'dad pa yin na//rigs kyi bu 
13b2(mdo sna tshogs): dang /rigs kyi bu mo dag phyag rgya'i sgo gang dag la bslabs na/chos thams cad la sgrib pa med pa'i mig dang shes pa la mkhas pa nyid rjes su thob par 'gyur/bcom ldan 'das kye ma bdag gis bsam pa'i tshogs gang 
13b3(mdo sna tshogs): dag dang /'jug pa sgrub pa gang dag gis go cha bgos pa sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i tshogs skye ba la bsam pa bsgrub pa'i tshogs la dgongs par 'gyur snyam mo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis pad ma dpal gyi snying po 
13b4(mdo sna tshogs): gzhon nur gyur pa'i bsam pa thugs su chud cing smon lam dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba'i tshogs sgrub pa thugs su chud nas de'i tshe phyogs bzhir rnam par gzigs so//phyogs bzhir gzigs pa na/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal 
13b5(mdo sna tshogs): gyi sgo nas 'od zer chen po bzhi byung ste/de dag byung nas 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa dag tu dong ngo//'od zer de dag kyang shing ldon pa'am//rtsig pa'am/ri'am/sa'am/bar snang gang du bab pa de dag 
13b6(mdo sna tshogs): thams cad gser gyi kha dog tu kha dog cig tu gyur to//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams thams cad du yang 'od zer de dag thogs pa med par rab tu dong ste/de ltar stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 
13b7(mdo sna tshogs): na 'od de dag gis reg pa'i sems can ji snyed pa de dag thams cad kyis rang gi lus ngag gser gyis lus lta bur shes so//gang dag 'dod chags kyi yongs su gdung bas yongs su gdungs pa dang /zhe sdang gi yongs 
13b8(mdo sna tshogs): su gdung bas yongs su gsungs pa dang /ti mug gi yongs su gdung bas yongs gsungs pa de dag thams cad kyi ni bdag nyid 'dod chags chung ba dang /zhe sdang chung ba dang /gti mug chung bar mngon par shes so//
14a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di ni sems can dmyal ba ji snyed yod pa de dag tu sems can skyes pa gang dag sdug bsngal che ba mi zad pa 'chi ba'i mthar byed pa'i tshor ba nyams su myong ba de 
14a2(mdo sna tshogs): dag thams cad kyis ni bcom ldan 'das kyi mthu dang /byang chub sems dpa' de'i sngon gyi smon lam gyi mthus bde ba snyam du shes par gyur to//stong gsum gyi stong chenapo'i 'jig rten gyi khams na las kyi sgrib pas 
14a3(mdo sna tshogs): bsgribs pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pas bsgribs pa dang /las kyi rnam par smin pa'i sgrib pas bsgribs par gyur ba'i sems can ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad kyis kyang /bcom ldan 'das gyi mthu dang /byang 
14a4(mdo sna tshogs): chub sems dpa' de'i sngon kyi smon lam gyis sgrib pa dang bral bar gyur to//de'i tshe sems can rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba cher gsal bar bya ba'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das kyis spu'i khung bu thams cad nas 'od zer dag phyung 
14a5(mdo sna tshogs): ste/'od zer de dag kyang shar phyogs su 'jig rten gyi khams tshad med pa dag 'das so//de bzhin du lho dang /nub dang /byang dang /steng dang /'og tu 'jig rten gyi khams tshad mad pa dag 'das so//des nas bcom ldan 'das 
14a6(mdo sna tshogs): kyis 'od zer de dag phyung nas gsung stsal ba'i sgra mdzad do//gsung bstsal ba'i sgra des kyang 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad mngon par go bar gyur to//de'i tshe shar phyogs su 'jig rten kyi khams grangs med pa 
14a7(mdo sna tshogs): dag 'das pa na 'jig rten gyi khams gdugs gcig pa zhes bya ba de na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rin po che'i bkod pa gcig pa zhes bya ba bzhugs te 'tsho zhing gzhes 
14a8(mdo sna tshogs): la chos kyang ston to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra pa bcom pa yang dag par rdzos pa'i sangs rgyas rin po cho'i bkod pa gcig pa des byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dra ba can gyi 'od 
14b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'di ni nga'i 'og tu bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro zhes bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu lung bstan to//de nas de bcom ldan 'das de bzhin

291b7(mdo sna tshogs): seng ge rnams byung ngo//stag rnams byung ngo//sprul rnams byung ngo//glang po rnams byung ngo//rdzu 'phrul chen po rnam par 'phrul pa ston to//ri la yang dkyil mo dkrung bcas te 'khod do//steng du dpag tshad nyi khri srid du
